		function _time(d){
			
			var hour = d.getHours();
			if (String(hour).length < 2) {hour = '0' + hour;}
			var min = d.getMinutes();
			if (String(min).length < 2) {min = '0' + min;}
			var sec = d.getSeconds();
			if (String(sec).length < 2) {sec = '0' + sec;}

    		return  hour + ":" + min + ":" + sec;
		}

		function _weekDay(d){
			var days = ["Domingo","Lunes","Martes","Miercoles","Jueves","Viernes","Sabado"];
			return days[d.getDay()];
		}

		function _typeDay(d){
			var typeDays = ["weekend","week","week","week","week","week","weekend"];
			return typeDays[d.getDay()];
		}
				
		function setNameNormalized(element) {
			if(element!=undefined && element!=null){
				element = element.replace(/-/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
				element = element.replace(/_/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
				return element;
			}
		}
		
		
		function _containPromotion(previous){
			 
			var result=false;
			if(previous!=undefined && previous!=null && previous.indexOf("promociones")!=-1 ){
				result=true;
			}
			return result;
		}

					sessionStorage.setItem("firstentrylogged", "s");	
				}

			
			if (datalayergeneral.page.extra.error != undefined && dataLayer.page.extra.error != undefined ) 
			{
				dataLayer.page.extra.error += datalayergeneral.page.extra.error;	
			}
			else
				{
				if (datalayergeneral.page.extra.error != undefined)
					{
						dataLayer.page.extra.error =datalayergeneral.page.extra.error;
					}
				}
			dataLayer.page.extra.category= datalayergeneral.page.extra.category;  
			dataLayer.page.extra.pageInstanceID =datalayergeneral.page.extra.pageInstanceID;
			dataLayer.component= datalayergeneral.component; 
			//transaction will always be calculated in datalayergeneral.js 
			dataLayer.transaction=datalayergeneral.transaction;
			
			
			
			dataLayer.page.notfound = '';
			dataLayer.content.share = '';   
			dataLayer.content.like = '';
			dataLayer.content.readlater = '';			
			
			if (datalayergeneral.page.section !=null && datalayergeneral.page.section !='') 
				{
					dataLayer.page.section = datalayergeneral.page.section;
					dataLayer.page.type = datalayergeneral.page.type;
					dataLayer.content.type = datalayergeneral.content.type;
					dataLayer.content.name = datalayergeneral.content.name;	
					dataLayer.content.author = datalayergeneral.content.author;	
					dataLayer.page.name = datalayergeneral.page.name;
					dataLayer.page.category = datalayergeneral.page.category;  
					dataLayer.page.hierarchy = datalayergeneral.page.hierarchy;
					dataLayer.page.subcategory1 = datalayergeneral.page.subcategory1; 
					
					if(datalayergeneral.products!=null && datalayergeneral.products!=''){   
						dataLayer.products=datalayergeneral.products;
					}
					
					if (datalayergeneral.page.subcategory2 !=null && datalayergeneral.page.subcategory2 !='') 
					{
						dataLayer.page.subcategory2=datalayergeneral.page.subcategory2;
						dataLayer.page.subcategory3=datalayergeneral.page.subcategory3;
					}
				}     
			
		    		    
            var d = new Date();
            dataLayer.page.extra.date = d.toDateString();
            dataLayer.global.hour = _time(d);
            dataLayer.global.day = _weekDay(d) 
            dataLayer.global.typeday = _typeDay(d);
			
           
           	dataLayer.page.referral = setNameNormalized(document.referrer);  
           	
           	
           	var containPromotion=_containPromotion(document.referrer);            
            if(containPromotion && (dataLayer.page.type == 'ficha producto simple' || dataLayer.page.type =='ficha producto variante')){             	
            	dataLayer.page.type= 'ficha producto promocion';             	
            }

         
            dataLayer.page.extra.originalURL  = document.location.href;
            
            //With this, the url of navigator always is stored in page url parameter
             dataLayer.page.url= setNameNormalized(window.location.href);

            if (dataLayer.page.category.error) {
                dataLayer.page.category.error += document.location.href;
                var s_errorType = dataLayer.page.extra.statusCode;      
                var s_originURL = document.location.href;
            }


            if(dataLayer.page.name.indexOf("error page") > -1 ){
                dataLayer.page.extra.destinationURL  = document.location.href;
                dataLayer.page.extra.category.error = "404 Error Page: " + document.location.href;
                var s_errorType = "404";
                var s_originURL = document.location.href;
                dataLayer.page.notfound = document.location.href;   
            }
            
            if (dataLayer.page.type !=null ) 
        	{
            	var paginaAnterior=sessionStorage.getItem("pagAnt");
            	
            	if(paginaAnterior!=null){
            		dataLayer.page.previous=paginaAnterior;
            	}else{
            		dataLayer.page.previous=setNameNormalized(document.referrer)!=null?setNameNormalized(document.referrer):""; 
            	}
            	sessionStorage.setItem("pagAnt", dataLayer.page.name);  
            	
            	
        	}else{
        		dataLayer.page.previous=setNameNormalized(document.referrer)!=null?setNameNormalized(document.referrer):""; 
        	}
            
            //Si estoy en una ficha de producto actualizo el product list position con el valor recuperado de la cookie
            if (dataLayer != null &&  dataLayer.products!=undefined && dataLayer.products[0]!=undefined && dataLayer.content.type  !=null &&   dataLayer.content.type=='producto'  ) 
         	{
            	dataLayer.products[0].productlistposition = getCookie("listPosition");            	
         	}
            
          //Complete field needed
          if (dataLayer.page.type == 'ficha producto simple' || dataLayer.page.type =='ficha producto variante'){
    		var sessionProducts = sessionStorage.getItem("sessionProducts");
    		if (sessionProducts != null){
    			
    			var parsed = JSON.parse(sessionProducts);

    			if (parsed!=null && parsed!=undefined){    				    			
	    			//JSON.parse doesn't return an array 
	    			var size = Object.keys(parsed).length;
	
	    			for (var i = 0; i<size; i++){
	    				var productId = dataLayer.products[0].id;
	    				var sessionProductId = parsed[i].id; 
	    				if (sessionProductId==productId){
	    					dataLayer.products[0].m2= parsed[i].m2!=undefined?parsed[i].m2:"";
	    					dataLayer.products[0].calculator= parsed[i].calculator!=null?parsed[i].calculator:"";	    					
	 	    			}
	    			}
    			}
    			  
    		}
    		
    		//Rating
    		var stars = $("#avg-rating-button").text(); //='4.8'; 
    		var opinions=$("#num-reviews-button").text(); //'(4)' 
    		var formattedRating = '';			

    		if (stars != undefined){				
    			formattedRating = stars;					
    		}
    		
    		if (opinions!=undefined){
    			opinions=opinions.replace('(','').replace(')','');				
    			if (formattedRating!='' && formattedRating!=undefined){
    				formattedRating= formattedRating+":"+opinions;
    			}else{
    				if (opinions!=undefined && opinions!=''){
    					formattedRating= ":"+opinions;				
    				}
    	
    			}	
    			
    			dataLayer.products[0].rating = formattedRating;
    		}	
    		
          }

          
                    
         //Evento buscador
         if (dataLayer.page.template == 'product list template'){
       	  var searchTerm = location.search.split('q=')[1];
      		  if (searchTerm!=undefined){
      			 var page = location.search.split('p=')[1];
      			 if (page==undefined){
      				 page='1';
      			 }
      			 dataLayer.page.name = dataLayer.page.name + ':page'+ page;
      		  }        		  
         }
          
          
      	$(document).on('click',	"#page-selection ul li", function(e) {
			var page = $(this).attr('data-lp');
			 var searchTerm = location.search.split('q=')[1];			 
			 if (searchTerm!=undefined){
				 if (page == undefined) {
					 page='1';
				 }else{
					 var name = DigitalData[0].page.name.split(":")[0];
					 DigitalData[0].page.name = name+':producto'+':page'+ page;	
				 }
       		 }

       			if (window.dbics!=undefined){
       			    DigitalData[0].page.template='';
       			    dbics.init();
    			}else{
    				console.error('Tag manager is not present in your js code or window.dbics is undefined');    						
       		 	}   
		});
          
          
            
            return dataLayer; 
		}		
	

		window.DigitalData = [_updateDataLayer()];
		
		function getCookie(name) {
			  var value = "; " + document.cookie;
			  var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
			  if (parts.length == 2) return parts.pop().split(";").shift();
			}
		
		
	

	</script>
